#
# «Диагностика» -- one press, everything the mod knows, as text.
#
# Generated. What it prints and why each block is here:
# `docs/pitfalls/diagnosis.md`. **No `[THIS...]` anywhere below**: a `debug_log`
# string cannot reach the item a walk stands on. Numbers come from the sixteen
# `_dv` scratch globals through the `_dg` readers; names are baked in here.
#
# Scope: country

	debug_log_date = yes
	error_log = "WTP the report is in debug.log, tag WTP, version 5. mods.bat -> «Забрать диагностику из игры» takes it out."
	debug_log = "WTP ==== BEGIN v5 ==== everything to the next END is one press. Take it out with mods.bat -> «Забрать диагностику из игры», or tools/diag.py."

# Everything chosen and everything the last plan counted. **A zero here is an
# answer**: pressed before a plan it prints zeros throughout, which says the pass
# never ran rather than that it found nothing.
# Scope: country

# The funnel. One walk, and the plan pays nothing for it: everything here is
# what the plan already parked on the locations.
#
# `w` a method won, `g` the gate would still open, `p` it was placed, `o` the
# best ordering it ever had -- against `bag_wtp_plan_band`, which is what a
# band admits. A good whose `o` never reaches 200 has only the last band.
# Scope: country

		# **Тумблеры считаются здесь, до разделения на стороны**, и это
		# сознательно: 2026-09-02 он сбросил их в «авто», а окно показало города
		# снова. Счёт по обеим сторонам различает «переменная вернулась» и «окно
		# врёт» -- по стороне это было бы неразличимо, потому что сброшенная
		# локация уходит на сельскую сторону вместе со своим счётчиком.
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = bag_wtp_force_town }
			change_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_diag_forced add = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = bag_wtp_force_rural }
			change_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_diag_forcedr add = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { bag_wtp_plan_is_town = yes }
			change_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_diag_towns add = 1 }
			# **Городская сторона и городской ранг -- разные вещи.** Тумблер
			# «сделать городом» переносит локацию на городскую сторону расчёта,
			# но ранга в игре не меняет -- а гильдия объявлена `town = yes` и в
			# `rural_settlement` не встанет никогда. 2026-09-02 весь симптом
			# оказался в этом зазоре, и это число его называет.
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { location_rank = location_rank:rural_settlement } }
				change_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_diag_realt add = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:bag_wtp_load < global_var:bag_wtp_plan_cap_urban }
				change_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_diag_freet add = 1 }
				if = { limit = { var:bag_wtp_pm1 > 0 } change_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_frt1 add = 1 } }
				if = { limit = { var:bag_wtp_pm2 > 0 } change_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_frt2 add = 1 } }
				if = { limit = { var:bag_wtp_pm3 > 0 } change_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_frt3 add = 1 } }
				if = { limit = { var:bag_wtp_pm4 > 0 } change_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_frt4 add = 1 } }
				if = { limit = { var:bag_wtp_pm5 > 0 } change_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_frt5 add = 1 } }
				if = { limit = { var:bag_wtp_pm6 > 0 } change_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_frt6 add = 1 } }
				if = { limit = { var:bag_wtp_pm7 > 0 } change_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_frt7 add = 1 } }
				if = { limit = { var:bag_wtp_pm8 > 0 } change_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_frt8 add = 1 } }
				if = { limit = { var:bag_wtp_pm9 > 0 } change_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_frt9 add = 1 } }
				if = { limit = { var:bag_wtp_pm10 > 0 } change_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_frt10 add = 1 } }
				if = { limit = { var:bag_wtp_pm11 > 0 } change_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_frt11 add = 1 } }

# One line per good, town and village apart, every counter uncompressed. The
# ladder is m -> a -> w -> g/p: the first zero is the stage the good is lost at,
# and the number before it is how many locations got that far.
# Scope: country
bag_wtp_diag_goods = {
	debug_log = "WTP GOODS legend: T=town R=village | m=methods(of them last-age) a=country has the advance w=a method won here r=of those, still has room g=the gate would still open p=placed o=best ordering it ever had (a band admits >= its own number) | ng=candidates that can make it q=quota n=placed both sides rgo=locations already yielding it as an RGO"
	debug_log = "WTP GOODS reading q: it is `PASS quota` + what the charters placed for this good - `rgo`, floored at 1, and then + 1 for each sweep of the open ladder -- which is `PASS open_sweeps`. So the quota the allocator enforced is q - open_sweeps, and a good with n at that number was stopped by its quota and not by the ground."
	debug_log = "WTP GOODS reading it: the first zero left to right is the stage. w>0 r=0 -- the ground filled up. w>0 r>0 g=0 -- the good or its building is already in every place that has room. g>0 p=0 -- the allocator never gave it a turn, so look at q, ng against the tiers, and o against the bands."
	bag_wtp_diag_good_1 = yes

# Room left over when the plan stopped, which is what tells a starved good from
# a full ground.

# What each pass of the allocator did. `sweeps=150` is a pass the guard
# cut off -- it wanted more rounds and was not given them -- and `placed` is the
# running total at the end of that pass, so the difference between two lines is
# what the second one put down.
# Scope: country
bag_wtp_diag_passes = {
	set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv1 value = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_global_variable = bag_wtp_passsw1 } set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv1 value = global_var:bag_wtp_passsw1 } }
	set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv2 value = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_global_variable = bag_wtp_passpl1 } set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv2 value = global_var:bag_wtp_passpl1 } }
	debug_log = "WTP P1 cover800 sweeps=[GuiScope.SetRoot(GetPlayer.MakeScope).ScriptValue('bag_wtp_dg1')|0]/150 placed=[GuiScope.SetRoot(GetPlayer.MakeScope).ScriptValue('bag_wtp_dg2')|0]"
	set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv1 value = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_global_variable = bag_wtp_passsw2 } set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv1 value = global_var:bag_wtp_passsw2 } }

# is the location itself, from `debug_log_scopes`; the `LG` lines under it are
# what was put there, one good a line -- which is the whole of the reported
# symptom in the one place it can be read.

		if = { limit = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_plan_goods target = goods:leather } NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_save_goods target = goods:leather } } } debug_log = "WTP LD +leather" }
		if = { limit = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_save_goods target = goods:liquor } NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_plan_goods target = goods:liquor } } } debug_log = "WTP LD -liquor" }
		if = { limit = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_plan_goods target = goods:liquor } NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_save_goods target = goods:liquor } } } debug_log = "WTP LD +liquor" }
		if = { limit = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_save_goods target = goods:masonry } NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_plan_goods target = goods:masonry } } } debug_log = "WTP LD -masonry" }
		if = { limit = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_plan_goods target = goods:masonry } NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_save_goods target = goods:masonry } } } debug_log = "WTP LD +masonry" }
		if = { limit = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_save_goods target = goods:naval_supplies } NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_plan_goods target = goods:naval_supplies } } } debug_log = "WTP LD -naval_supplies" }
		if = { limit = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_plan_goods target = goods:naval_supplies } NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_save_goods target = goods:naval_supplies } } } debug_log = "WTP LD +naval_supplies" }
		if = { limit = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_save_goods target = goods:paper } NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_plan_goods target = goods:paper } } } debug_log = "WTP LD -paper" }
		if = { limit = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_plan_goods target = goods:paper } NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_save_goods target = goods:paper } } } debug_log = "WTP LD +paper" }
		if = { limit = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_save_goods target = goods:porcelain } NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_plan_goods target = goods:porcelain } } } debug_log = "WTP LD -porcelain" }
		if = { limit = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_plan_goods target = goods:porcelain } NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_save_goods target = goods:porcelain } } } debug_log = "WTP LD +porcelain" }

		if = { limit = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_plan_goods target = goods:pottery } NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_save_goods target = goods:pottery } } } debug_log = "WTP LD +pottery" }
		if = { limit = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_save_goods target = goods:slaves_goods } NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_plan_goods target = goods:slaves_goods } } } debug_log = "WTP LD -slaves_goods" }
		if = { limit = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_plan_goods target = goods:slaves_goods } NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_save_goods target = goods:slaves_goods } } } debug_log = "WTP LD +slaves_goods" }
		if = { limit = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_save_goods target = goods:steel } NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_plan_goods target = goods:steel } } } debug_log = "WTP LD -steel" }
		if = { limit = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_plan_goods target = goods:steel } NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_save_goods target = goods:steel } } } debug_log = "WTP LD +steel" }
		if = { limit = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_save_goods target = goods:tar } NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_plan_goods target = goods:tar } } } debug_log = "WTP LD -tar" }
		if = { limit = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_plan_goods target = goods:tar } NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_save_goods target = goods:tar } } } debug_log = "WTP LD +tar" }
		if = { limit = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_save_goods target = goods:tools } NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_plan_goods target = goods:tools } } } debug_log = "WTP LD -tools" }
		if = { limit = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_plan_goods target = goods:tools } NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_save_goods target = goods:tools } } } debug_log = "WTP LD +tools" }
		if = { limit = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_save_goods target = goods:weaponry } NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_plan_goods target = goods:weaponry } } } debug_log = "WTP LD -weaponry" }
		if = { limit = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_plan_goods target = goods:weaponry } NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = bag_wtp_save_goods target = goods:weaponry } } } debug_log = "WTP LD +weaponry" }
	}
	set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv1 value = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_global_variable = bag_wtp_diag_n } set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv1 value = global_var:bag_wtp_diag_n } }
	set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv2 value = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_global_variable = bag_wtp_plan_found } set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv2 value = global_var:bag_wtp_plan_found } }
	debug_log = "WTP LOCS printed=[GuiScope.SetRoot(GetPlayer.MakeScope).ScriptValue('bag_wtp_dg1')|0] of=[GuiScope.SetRoot(GetPlayer.MakeScope).ScriptValue('bag_wtp_dg2')|0] cap=200 -- a cap is never silently the answer"
}

# The single-good ranking's own answer, for the case where the plan is right and
# the table it is read against is not. A row is a province definition.
# Scope: country
bag_wtp_diag_ranking = {
	set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_diag_n value = 0 }
	ordered_in_global_list = {
		variable = bag_wtp_ranked
		order_by = bag_wtp_rank_order
		max = 25
		check_range_bounds = no
		change_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_diag_n add = 1 }
		set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv1 value = 0 }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = bag_wtp_rank } set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv1 value = var:bag_wtp_rank } }
		set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv2 value = 0 }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = bag_wtp_bonus } set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv2 value = var:bag_wtp_bonus } }
		set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv3 value = 0 }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = bag_wtp_out } set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv3 value = var:bag_wtp_out } }
		set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv4 value = 0 }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = bag_wtp_bonus_rural } set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv4 value = var:bag_wtp_bonus_rural } }
		set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv5 value = 0 }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = bag_wtp_out_rural } set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv5 value = var:bag_wtp_out_rural } }
		set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv6 value = 0 }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = bag_wtp_end_bonus } set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv6 value = var:bag_wtp_end_bonus } }
		set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv7 value = 0 }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = bag_wtp_end_out } set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv7 value = var:bag_wtp_end_out } }
		set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv8 value = 0 }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = bag_wtp_mid_bonus } set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv8 value = var:bag_wtp_mid_bonus } }
		set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv9 value = 0 }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = bag_wtp_mid_age } set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv9 value = var:bag_wtp_mid_age } }
		debug_log_scopes = no
		debug_log = "WTP R rank=[GuiScope.SetRoot(GetPlayer.MakeScope).ScriptValue('bag_wtp_dg1')|0] now_town=[GuiScope.SetRoot(GetPlayer.MakeScope).ScriptValue('bag_wtp_dg2')|0]/[GuiScope.SetRoot(GetPlayer.MakeScope).ScriptValue('bag_wtp_dg3')|0] now_village=[GuiScope.SetRoot(GetPlayer.MakeScope).ScriptValue('bag_wtp_dg4')|0]/[GuiScope.SetRoot(GetPlayer.MakeScope).ScriptValue('bag_wtp_dg5')|0] end_town=[GuiScope.SetRoot(GetPlayer.MakeScope).ScriptValue('bag_wtp_dg6')|0]/[GuiScope.SetRoot(GetPlayer.MakeScope).ScriptValue('bag_wtp_dg7')|0] mid=[GuiScope.SetRoot(GetPlayer.MakeScope).ScriptValue('bag_wtp_dg8')|0] mid_age=[GuiScope.SetRoot(GetPlayer.MakeScope).ScriptValue('bag_wtp_dg9')|0]"
	}
	set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv1 value = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_global_variable = bag_wtp_diag_n } set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv1 value = global_var:bag_wtp_diag_n } }
	set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv2 value = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_global_variable = bag_wtp_found } set_global_variable = { name = bag_wtp_dv2 value = global_var:bag_wtp_found } }
	debug_log = "WTP ROWS printed=[GuiScope.SetRoot(GetPlayer.MakeScope).ScriptValue('bag_wtp_dg1')|0] of=[GuiScope.SetRoot(GetPlayer.MakeScope).ScriptValue('bag_wtp_dg2')|0] cap=25"
}
